====================================================================\n        bind(KeyCode::Right, cmd_alt, Command::NextTab),\n        bind(KeyCode::Left, cmd_alt, Command::PrevTab),\n\n        // ====================================================================\n        // Layout: Focus Groups\n        // ====================================================================\n        bind(KeyCode::Tab, ctrl, Command::FocusNextGroup),\n        bind(KeyCode::Tab, ctrl_shift, Command::FocusPrevGroup),\n        bind(KeyCode::Char('1'), cmd_shift, Command::FocusGroup1),\n        bind(KeyCode::Char('2'), cmd_shift, Command::FocusGroup2),\n        bind(KeyCode::Char('3'), cmd_shift, Command::FocusGroup3),\n        bind(KeyCode::Char('4'), cmd_shift, Command::FocusGroup4),\n\n        // Numpad group focus (no modifiers needed)\n        bind(KeyCode::Numpad1, none, Command::FocusGroup1),\n        bind(KeyCode::Numpad2, none, Command::FocusGroup2),\n        bind(KeyCode::Numpad3, none, Command::FocusGroup3),\n        bind(KeyCode::Numpad4, none, Command::FocusGroup4),\n        bind(KeyCode::NumpadAdd, none, Command::SplitVertical),\n        bind(KeyCode::NumpadSubtract, none, Command::SplitHorizontal),\n\n        // ====================================================================\n        // Basic Navigation (no selection)\n        // ====================================================================\n        bind(KeyCode::Up, none, Command::MoveCursorUp),\n        bind(KeyCode::Down, none, Command::MoveCursorDown),\n        bind(KeyCode::Left, none, Command::MoveCursorLeft),\n        bind(KeyCode::Right, none, Command::MoveCursorRight),\n        bind(KeyCode::Home, none, Command::MoveCursorLineStart),\n        bind(KeyCode::End, none, Command::MoveCursorLineEnd),\n        bind(KeyCode::PageUp, none, Command::PageUp),\n        bind(KeyCode::PageDown, none, Command::PageDown),\n\n        // Word navigation (Alt+Arrow)\n        bind(KeyCode::Left, alt, Command::MoveCursorWordLeft),\n        bind(KeyCode::Right, alt, Command::MoveCursorWordRight),\n\n        //    bind(KeyCode::Up, shift, Command::MoveCursorUpWithSelection),\n        bind(KeyCode::Down, shift, Command::MoveCursorDownWithSelection),\n        bind(KeyCode::Left, shift, Command::MoveCursorLeftWithSelection),\n        bind(KeyCode::Right, shift, Command::MoveCursorRightWithSelection),\n        bind(KeyCode::Home, shift, Command::MoveCursorLineStartWithSelection),\n        bind(KeyCode::End, shift, Command::MoveCursorLineEndWithSelection),\n        bind(KeyCode::PageUp, shift, Command::PageUpWithSelection),\n        bind(KeyCode::PageDown, shift, Command::PageDownWithSelection),\n\n        // Word navigation with selection (Alt+Shift+Arrow)\n        bind(KeyCode::Left, alt_shift, Command::MoveCursorWordLeftWithSelection),\n        bind(KeyCode::Right, alt_shift, Command::MoveCursorWordRightWithSelection),\n\n        // ====================================================================\n        // Editing\n        //     bind(KeyCode::Delete, none, Command::DeleteForward),\n        bind(KeyCode::Backspace, alt, Command::DeleteWordBackward),\n        bind(KeyCode::Backspace, cmd, Command::DeleteLine),\n        bind(KeyCode::Space, none, Command::InsertTab), // Will need context: InsertChar(' ') normally\n\n        // Tab handling - these will need context conditions\n        // Tab with selection -> IndentLines\n        // Tab without selection -> InsertTab\n        // For now, default to InsertTab, context system will refine this\n        bind(KeyCode::Tab, none, Command::InsertTab),\n        bind(KeyCode::Tab, shift, Command::UnindentLines),\n\n        // ====================================================================\n        // Expand/Shrink Selection (Option+Up/Down)\n        // ====================================================================\n        bind(KeyCode::Up, alt, Command::ExpandSelection),\n        bind(KeyCode::Down, alt, Command::ShrinkSelection),\n\n        // ====================================================================\n        // Escape (smart clear)\n        // ====================================================================\n        bind(KeyCode::Escape, none, Command::EscapeSmartClear),\n    ];\n\n    // Platform-specific additions\n    #[cfg(target_os = \"macos\")]\n    {\n        // macOS: Cmd+Arrow for line start/end\n        bindings.push(bind(KeyCode::Left, cmd, Command::MoveCursorLineStart));\n        bindings.push(bind(KeyCode::Right, cmd, Command::MoveCursorLineEnd));\n        bindings.push(bind(KeyCode::Left, cmd_shift, Command::MoveCursorLineStartWithSelection));\n        bindings.push(bind(KeyCode::Right, cmd_shift, Command::MoveCursorLineEndWithSelection));\n    }\n\n    #[cfg(not(target_os = \"macos\"))]\n    {\n        // Windows/Linux: Ctrl+Home/End for document start/end\n        bindings.push(bind(KeyCode::Home, ctrl, Command::MoveCursorDocumentStart));\n        bindings.push(bind(KeyCode::End, ctrl, Command::MoveCursorDocumentEnd));\n        bindings.push(bind(KeyCode::Home, ctrl_shift, Command::MoveCursorDocumentStartWithSelection));\n        bindings.push(bind(KeyCode::End, ctrl_shift, Command::MoveCursorDocumentEndWithSelection));\n    }\n\n    // Document start/end with Ctrl on all platforms (works on macOS too)\n    bindings.push(bind(KeyCode::Home, ctrl, Command::MoveCursorDocumentStart));\n    bindings.push(bind(KeyCode::End, ctrl, Command::MoveCursorDocumentEnd));\n    bindings.push(bind(KeyCode::Home, ctrl_shift, Command::MoveCursorDocumentStartWithSelection));\n    bindings.push(bind(KeyCode::End, ctrl_shift, Command::MoveCursorDocumentEndWithSelection));\n\n    bindings\n}\n\n/// Helper to create a keybinding\nfn bind(key: KeyCode, mods: Modifiers, command: Command) -> Keybinding {\n    Keybinding::new(Keystroke::new(key, mods), command)\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_default_bindings_not_empty() {\n        let bindings = default_bindings();\n        assert!(!bindings.is_empty());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_has_save_binding() {\n        let bindings = default_bindings();\n        let has_save = bindings.iter().any(|b| b.command == Command::SaveFile);\n        assert!(has_save, \"Should have SaveFile binding\");\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_has_undo_binding() {\n        let bindings = default_bindings();\n        let has_undo = bindings.iter().any(|b| b.command == Command::Undo);\n        assert!(has_undo, \"Should have Undo binding\");\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_has_cursor_movement() {\n        let bindings = default_bindings();\n        let has_up = bindings.iter().any(|b| b.command == Command::MoveCursorUp);\n        let has_down = bindings.iter().any(|b| b.command == Command::MoveCursorDown);\n        let has_left = bindings.iter().any(|b| b.command == Command::MoveCursorLeft);\n        let has_right = bindings.iter().any(|b| b.command == Command::MoveCursorRight);\n        \n        assert!(has_up && has_down && has_left && has_right, \"Should have all arrow key bindings\");\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_platform_cmd_key() {\n        let cmd = Modifiers::cmd();\n        \n        #[cfg(target_os = \"macos\")]\n        assert!(cmd.meta(), \"cmd() should be META on macOS\");\n        \n        #[cfg(not(target_os = \"macos\"))]\n        assert!(cmd.ctrl(), \"cmd() should be CTRL on non-macOS\");\n    }\n}\n"
}

### Summary: Phases 1-3 Complete

#### Files Created

| File | Purpose |
|------|---------|
| [src/keymap/mod.rs](file://~/code/rust-editor/src/keymap/mod.rs) | Module root, public API exports |
| [src/keymap/types.rs](file://~/code/rust-editor/src/keymap/types.rs) | `Modifiers`, `KeyCode`, `Keystroke` types |
| [src/keymap/winit_adapter.rs](file://~/code/rust-editor/src/keymap/winit_adapter.rs) | `keystroke_from_winit()` conversion |
| [src/keymap/command.rs](file://~/code/rust-editor/src/keymap/command.rs) | `Command` enum with 60+ variants, `to_msgs()` |
| [src/keymap/binding.rs](file://~/code/rust-editor/src/keymap/binding.rs) | `Keybinding` struct (single + chord) |
| [src/keymap/keymap.rs](file://~/code/rust-editor/src/keymap/keymap.rs) | `Keymap` with lookup, chord support |
| [src/keymap/defaults.rs](file://~/code/rust-editor/src/keymap/defaults.rs) | `default_bindings()` with platform detection |
| [src/keymap/tests.rs](file://~/code/rust-editor/src/keymap/tests.rs) | Integration tests |

#### Key Features

- **Platform-aware `Modifiers::cmd()`** - Returns META on macOS, CTRL elsewhere
- **42 passing tests** covering types, lookup, chords, defaults
- **`Command::to_msgs()`** converts commands to Elm-style messages
- **Chord support** in `Keymap::handle_keystroke()` with `KeyAction::{Execute, AwaitMore, NoMatch}`
- **Display strings** for keybindings (⌘S on macOS, Ctrl+S elsewhere)

#### Next Steps (Phase 4+)

The keymap is ready to be integrated with `input.rs`. Phase 4 will:
1. Add keymap to `App` struct
2. Try keymap lookup first for simple commands
3. Fall back to existing match for complex/context-dependent behaviors

super::types::{KeyCode, Keystroke, Modifiers};\n\n/// Root structure of a keymap YAML file\n#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]\npub struct KeymapConfig {\n    pub bindings: Vec<BindingConfig>,\n}\n\n/// A single binding entry from YAML\n#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]\npub struct BindingConfig {\n    pub key: String,\n    pub command: String,\n    #[serde(default)]\n    pub platform: Option<String>,\n}\n\n/// Load keybindings from a YAML file\npub fn load_keymap_file(path: &Path) -> Result<Vec<Keybinding>, KeymapError> {\n    let content = std::fs::read_to_string(path)\n        .map_err(|e| KeymapError::IoError(e.to_string()))?;\n    \n    parse_keymap_yaml(&content)\n}\n\n/// Parse keybindings from YAML string\npub fn parse_keymap_yaml(yaml: &str) -> Result<Vec<Keybinding>, KeymapError> {\n    let config: KeymapConfig = serde_yaml::from_str(yaml)\n        .map_err(|e| KeymapError::ParseError(e.to_string()))?;\n    \n    let current_platform = get_current_platform();\n    let mut bindings = Vec::new();\n    \n    for entry in config.bindings {\n        // Skip if platform-specific and doesn't match current platform\n        if let Some(ref platform) = entry.platform {\n            if platform != current_platform {\n                continue;\n            }\n        }\n        \n        let keystroke = parse_key_string(&entry.key)?;\n        let command = parse_command(&entry.command)?;\n        bindings.push(Keybinding::new(keystroke, command));\n    }\n    \n    Ok(bindings)\n}\n\n/// Parse a key string like \"cmd+shift+s\" into a Keystroke\npub fn parse_key_string(key_str: &str) -> Result<Keystroke, KeymapError> {\n    let parts: Vec<&str> = key_str.split('+').collect();\n    \n    if parts.is_empty() {\n        return Err(KeymapError::InvalidKey(key_str.to_string()));\n    }\n    \n    let mut mods = Modifiers::NONE;\n    let mut key_part = None;\n    \n    for part in parts {\n        let part_lower = part.to_lowercase();\n        match part_lower.as_str() {\n            \"cmd\" => {\n                // Platform command key\n                mods = mods | Modifiers::cmd();\n            }\n            \"ctrl\" | \"control\" => {\n                mods = mods | Modifiers::CTRL;\n            }\n            \"shift\" => {\n                mods = mods | Modifiers::SHIFT;\n            }\n            \"alt\" | \"option\" | \"opt\" => {\n                mods = mods | Modifiers::ALT;\n            }\n            \"meta\" | \"super\" | \"win\" => {\n                mods = mods | Modifiers::META;\n            }\n            _ => {\n                // This should be the key itself\n                if key_part.is_some() {\n                    return Err(KeymapError::InvalidKey(format!(\n                        \"Multiple keys in binding: {}\",\n                        key_str\n                    )));\n                }\n                key_part = Some(parse_key_code(&part_lower)?);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    \n    let key = key_part.ok_or_else(|| {\n        KeymapError::InvalidKey(format!(\"No key found in binding: {}\", key_str))\n    })?;\n    \n    Ok(Keystroke::new(key, mods))\n}\n\n/// Parse a key code from string\nfn parse_key_code(key: &str) -> Result<KeyCode, KeymapError> {\n    // Single character\n    if key.len() == 1 {\n        let c = key.chars().next().unwrap();\n        return Ok(KeyCode::Char(c.to_ascii_lowercase()));\n    }\n    \n    // Named keys\n    match key {\n        \"enter\" | \"return\" => Ok(KeyCode::Enter),\n        \"escape\" | \"esc\" => Ok(KeyCode::Escape),\n        \"tab\" => Ok(KeyCode::Tab),\n        \"backspace\" | \"back\" => Ok(KeyCode::Backspace),\n        \"delete\" | \"del\" => Ok(KeyCode::Delete),\n        \"space\" => Ok(KeyCode::Space),\n        \n        \"up\" | \"arrowup\" => Ok(KeyCode::Up),\n        \"down\" | \"arrowdown\" => Ok(KeyCode::Down),\n        \"left\" | \"arrowleft\" => Ok(KeyCode::PageUp),\n        \"pagedown\" | \"pgdown\" | \"pgdn\" => Ok(KeyCode::PageDown),\n        \"insert\" | \"ins\" => Ok(KeyCode::Insert),\n        \n        // Function keys\n        \"f1\" => Ok(KeyCode::F(1)),\n        \"f2\" => Ok(KeyCode::F(2)),\n        \"f3\" => Ok(KeyCode::F(3)),\n        \"f4\" => Ok(KeyCode::F(4)),\n        \"f5\" => Ok(KeyCode::F(5)),\n        \"f6\" =>  \"numpad5\" | \"num5\" => Ok(KeyCode::Numpad5),\n        \"numpad6\" | \"num6\" => Ok(KeyCode::Numpad6),\n        \"numpad7\" | \"num7\" => Ok(KeyCode::Numpad7),\n        \"numpad8\" | \"num8\" => Ok(KeyCode::Numpad8),\n        \"numpad9\" | \"num9\" => Ok(KeyCode::Numpad9),\n        \"numpad_add\" | \"numadd\" | \"numplus\" => Ok(KeyCode::NumpadAdd),\n        \"numpad_subtract\" | \"numsub\" | \"numminus\" => Ok(KeyCode::NumpadSubtract),\n        \"numpad_multiply\" | \"nummul\" => Ok(KeyCode::NumpadMultiply),\n        \"numpad_divide\" | \"numdiv\" => Ok(KeyCode::NumpadDivide),\n        \"numpad_enter\" | \"numenter\" => Ok(KeyCode::NumpadEnter),\n        \"numpad_decimal\" | \"numdot\" => Ok(KeyCode::NumpadDecimal),\n        \n        _ => Err(KeymapError::InvalidKey(format!(\"Unknown key: {}\", key))),\n    }\n}\n\n/// Parse a command name string into a Command enum\nfn parse_command(cmd: &str) -> Result<Command, KeymapError> {\n parse_keymap_yaml(yaml).unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(bindings.len(), 2);\n        assert_eq!(bindings[0].command, Command::SaveFile);\n        assert_eq!(bindings[1].command, Command::Undo);\n    }\n platform: macos\n\"#;\n        \n        let bindings = parse_keymap_yaml(yaml).unwrap();\n        \n        // On macOS, should have 2 bindings; on other platforms, 1\n        #[cfg(target_os = \"macos\")]\n        assert_eq!(bindings.len(), 2);\n        \n        #[cfg(not(target_os = \"macos\"))]\n        assert_eq!(bindings.len(), 1);\n    }\n    \n    #[test]\n    fn test_parse_numpad() {\n        let stroke = parse_key_string(\"numpad1\").unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(stroke.key, KeyCode::Numpad1);\n        \n        let stroke = parse_key_string(\"numpad_add\").unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(stroke.key, KeyCode::NumpadAdd);\n    }\n}\n"

### Summary - Phases 1-5 Complete

#### Files Created/Modified

| File | Purpose |
|------|---------|
| [keymap.yaml](file://~/code/rust-editor/keymap.yaml) | Default keybindings in YAML format |
| [src/keymap/mod.rs](file://~/code/rust-editor/src/keymap/mod.rs) | Module root with exports |
| [src/keymap/types.rs](file://~/code/rust-editor/src/keymap/types.rs) | `Modifiers`, `KeyCode`, `Keystroke` |
| [src/keymap/winit_adapter.rs](file://~/code/rust-editor/src/keymap/winit_adapter.rs) | `keystroke_from_winit()` conversion |
| [src/keymap/command.rs](file://~/code/rust-editor/src/keymap/command.rs) | `Command` enum (60+ variants), `to_msgs()` |
| [src/keymap/binding.rs](file://~/code/rust-editor/src/keymap/binding.rs) | `Keybinding` struct |
| [src/keymap/keymap.rs](file://~/code/rust-editor/src/keymap/keymap.rs) | `Keymap` with lookup + chord support |
| [src/keymap/config.rs](file://~/code/rust-editor/src/keymap/config.rs) | YAML parsing |
| [src/keymap/defaults.rs](file://~/code/rust-editor/src/keymap/defaults.rs) | `load_default_keymap()` |
| [src/keymap/tests.rs](file://~/code/rust-editor/src/keymap/tests.rs) | Integration tests |
| [src/runtime/app.rs](file://~/code/rust-editor/src/runtime/app.rs) | Bridge integration |
| [src/commands.rs](file://~/code/rust-editor/src/commands.rs) | `CommandId::to_keymap_command()` mapping |

#### Key Features Implemented

1. **YAML-based keybindings** - Edit `keymap.yaml` instead of code
2. **Platform-aware `cmd` modifier** - Maps to ⌘ on macOS, Ctrl elsewhere
3. **Bridge architecture** - Keymap tried first, falls back to `input.rs` for complex behaviors
4. **53 passing tests** covering types, parsing, lookup, chords
5. **Chord support** - `KeyAction::AwaitMore` for multi-key sequences

#### What's Still in `input.rs`

Complex context-dependent behaviors that need the Phase 6 context system:
- Selection collapse on arrow keys
- Tab insert vs indent based on selection
- Escape cascade (multi-cursor → selection → nothing)
- Option double-tap for multi-cursor
